LP phase algorithm:
```
def lp_iteration(a, M):
  A = build_A(a) # maybe reuse? build each iter.
  res = linprog(-ones, A_ub=A, b_ub=M, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
  if success: b=res.x; b=max(b,0); sumb=sum; if sumb>0 return b/sumb
```

Let's design a robust `improve(a, time_limit, rng, mode)`:
1. Normalize. Evaluate M exact/FFT. best.
2. Use while time < limit:
   - Fast exact objective via FFT. Update best.
   - If stagnation, choose strategy:
      * if iteration % 2==0 maybe LP direction (if n ≤ N_LP and enough time). Solve LP (primal) with current a,M. If success, evaluate candidate direction g. Also maybe solve dual and gradient direction g_grad. Add to candidates list.
      * generate gradient top-lags direction with λ_top from active top min(64,?) weights using softmax temperature: weights = exp((c - M)/tau) where tau = M*0.05 or adaptive. Compute grad. Direction = project(a - step*grad). Alternatively minimize weighted sum: use small line search.
      * random: Gaussian, block scaling, smoothing.
   - For each candidate direction/direct candidate, perform line search (maybe if direction vector sum1) to find α that maximizes improvement. For direct candidates just take best.
   - Pick best next a; if not improving, apply perturbation or reset to best with larger random.
3. Return best.

But line search for each candidate can be expensive. Conv eval FFT O(512 log512) cheap (~0.0005s?). 1300s / maybe 1e6 evals possible. But LP dominates. We can cap candidates.

Implement `_conv_full(a)`:
```
def _conv_full(a):
    n=len(a)
    nfft=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    f=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f,nfft)[:2*n-1]
    if c.dtype? irfft float64
    c[c<0 & c>-1e-12]=0? Better np.maximum(c,0)
    c[~finite]=0
    return c
```

```
def _weighted_grad(a, w):
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1
    if w sparse? maybe dense
    wr = w[::-1]
    nfft=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); fw=np.fft.rfft(wr,nfft)
    cr=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,nfft)[:3*n-2]
    indices=2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
    return 2.0*cr[indices]
```
If `w` all zeros? return zeros.

`_project_simplex(v)`:
Standard:
```
def _project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=len(v)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1)-1) > (cssv-target))[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return v/target if target>0?
    theta=(cssv[rho[-1]]-target)/ (rho[-1]+1)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
    return w
```
But if sum(v) can be >target and all positive. Standard works. Need ensure target=1. If v all negative, raises? Return uniform. Simpler: if not finite or target: uniform. If max(v)<target? Then theta negative? Actually sum<target, max possible sum target with nonnegative? Need add mass; standard projection to sum target when sum(v)<target sets theta negative so all v-theta positive and sum target. Formula with cssv-target negative maybe rho empty? Let's implement robust:
- If not finite: uniform.
- Sort desc, cssv. Need find rho where `u*(1:n)- (cssv-target) >0`. If target sum=1. If sum(v)<1, all entries may positive after shift; rho could be empty? For simple sum=0, u=0, cssv-target=-1, u*(i-1)-cssv =0+1 >0? Actually formula: condition u_i * i > (cssv_target? Let's use known:
```
def simplex_projection(v, s):
    n=len(v); u=sort(v)[::-1]; cssv=cumsum(u); idx=np.arange(1,n+1); rho=np.where((cssv - s) / idx < u)[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.full(n,s/n)
    theta=(cssv[rho[-1]]-s)/(rho[-1]+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Condition derivation: maximize ∑ w_i w_i with w_i ≥v_i -θ? Standard: find ρ max such that u_ρ > (∑_{1..ρ}u_i - s)/ρ. Use `<`. Test v=[0,0], s=1: u=[0,0], cssv=[0,0], theta condition: (0-1)/1=-1 <0 true; (0-1)/2=-0.5<0 true; ρ=2? rho[-1]=1, theta=(0-1)/2=-0.5, v-theta=0.5. good. If v=[2,2], s=1: u=[2,2], cssv=[2,4]; condition (2-1)/1=1<2 true; (4-1)/2=1.5<2 true; ρ=1 theta=(4-1)/2=1.5; result [0.5,0.5]. Good. Pseudo gradient phase:
```
def _gradient_search(a, deadline, rng, mode='soft', max_no_improve=...):
  n=len(a); best=a; Mbest=conv_max(a); sigma=0.03
  patience=0
  while time<deadline:
    c=conv_full(a); M=c.max()
    if M<Mbest*(1-1e-12): best...; patience=0; sigma=max(0.003, sigma*0.98)
    else: patience+=1
    if patience>50:
       # random or from best
       if rng.random()<0.5: a=project(best+normal(0,sigma))
       else: a=smooth_init...
       sigma*=1.5; patience=0
    weights = _active_weights(c,M) # maybe combine
    grad = _weighted_grad(a, weights)
    best_point=a; best_M=M
    for eta in step list:
       v=a-eta*grad; p=project; Mnew=conv_max; update
       # multiplicative
       gmean=grad.mean(); if >0: v=a*np.exp(-eta*grad/gmean); v/=sum; update
    # if no improvement, try topK weights with equal weights
    if best_M<M*(1-1e-12): a=best_point
    else: patience += 2
  return best,Mbest
```

def _local_refine(a, t_limit, rng, use_lp=True, lp_n_limit=512, allow_random_init=False):
    n=len(a)
    a=_normalize(a.copy())
    c=_conv_full(a); M=c.max()
    best=a.copy(); best_M=M
    patience=0; pmax=30
    sigma=0.02
    iter=0
    lp_count=0
    use_lp = use_lp and n <= lp_n_limit and time.monotonic() < t_limit - 5
    # We need c current in loop.
    while time.monotonic() < t_limit:
        iter+=1
        # update best
        if M < best_M*(1-1e-12) or best_M is None:
            best=a.copy(); best_M=M; patience=0; sigma=max(0.003, sigma*0.97)
        else:
            patience += 1
        if patience > pmax:
            # restart from best with perturbation
            if rng.random() < 0.7:

            else:

                a = _project_simplex(0.5*a + 0.5*random smooth)

            c=_conv_full(a); M=c.max()
            continue
        best_point=a; best_cand_M=M
        # Optional LP
        if use_lp and iter % 2 == 0 and time.monotonic() < t_limit - 5 and np.linalg.norm? no:
            res = _solve_lp(a, M, t_limit, rng)
            if res is not None:
                dirb, weights = res
                # line search dirb
                if dirb is not None:
                    # choose alphas maybe adapt

        # Actually if LP didn't improve, try gradient top weights

               w=_top_weights(c,K)
               grad=...
               for eta in steps:

            c=_conv_full(a); M=_conv_max? maybe use c max; M=best_cand_M
        else:
            # if best_point a (no change), continue; patience done above
            pass
        if time > breaks? 
    return best,best_M

def _top_weights(c, K):

    idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1.0/K
    return w

Now, `_solve_lp` details:
```
def _solve_lp(a, M, deadline, rng):
  n=len(a)
  if n>MAX_LP_N: return None
  if M <=0 or time > deadline -2: return None
  A=_build_A(a)
  A = A / M
  eps = 1e-4 maybe
  tie = rng.normal(0,1,n); tie -= tie.mean(); tie /= (np.std(tie)+1e-12)
  c_obj = -1.0 + 1e-5*tie # (minimize)
  b_ub = np.ones(2*n-1)
  try:
    res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': max(0.1, min(3, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.25))})
  except: ...
  dirb=None; weights=None
  b=res.x if success else None
  if b: b=np.clip(b,0,None); s=b.sum(); if s>1e-12: dirb=b/s
  # optional solve dual if time, using A without scaling? dual of unscaled? We need weights satisfying A_orig^T λ ≥1. Use A_orig before scaling.
  if time < deadline - 4 and n<=300? maybe solve dual occasionally (if dirb not improving)
```

Upsample function:
```
def _resample(a, m):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0 or m<=0: return np.ones(m)/m
    if n==m: return normalize(a)
    if m < n:
       # binning sum over blocks proportional. Use np.array_split? Simpler: use cumulative sum interp.
       # For coarse, sample continuous shape via cumulative integral? Since a values represent heights, integration not simple. Use linear interpolation of cumulative? For mass-preserving resampling, use cumulative distribution function inverse: positions around 0.5/n? 
       # Easier: np.interp on index; then normalize. mass approximate.
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    return normalize(np.interp(x_new,x_old,a))
```
For mass approx not exact; normalize. Works.
For m > n, endpoint maybe includes a0 maybe 0. Good.
Alternatively use FFT resampling to preserve continuous shape; linear interp enough.

Pseudo code for `_local_refine`:
```
def _local_refine(a, deadline, rng, use_lp=True, lp_every=2, top_steps= (0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0)):
    n=len(a)
    if n < 2: a=np.ones(2)/2
    a=_normalize(a)
    c=_conv_full(a); M=float(c.max())
    best=a.copy(); best_M=M
    patience=0; sigma=0.03
    iter=0
    cur_c = c
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        iter+=1
        if M < best_M*(1-1e-13):
            best=a.copy(); best_M=M; patience=0; sigma=max(0.004,sigma*0.98)
        else:
            patience += 1
        if patience > 40:
            # restart
            if rng.random()<0.75:
                a = _project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0, sigma, n))
            else:
                a = _project_simplex(_random_smooth(n, rng) + 0.5*best)
            c=_conv_full(a); M=float(c.max()); patience=0; sigma=min(0.4, sigma*1.4); continue
        best_point=None; best_M_new=M; best_c_new=c
        tried = 0
        # LP
        if use_lp and (iter % lp_every == 0):
            res=_solve_lp(a,M,deadline,rng,with_dual=(iter%4==2))
            if res:
                dirb,res_w=res
                tried=True
                if dirb is not None:
                    alphas = [0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0] if n<300 else [0.1,0.2,0.4,0.6,1.0]
                    for alpha in alphas:
                        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                        cand = a + alpha*(dirb-a)
                        cc=_conv_full(cand); mm=float(cc.max())
                        if mm < best_M_new*(1-1e-13):
                            best_M_new=mm; best_point=cand.copy(); best_c_new=cc
                if res_w is not None:
                    grad=_weighted_grad(a,res_w, n?)
                    tried_g=True
                    for eta in [0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5]:
                        cand = _project_simplex(a - eta*grad)
                        cc=...; update
                    gm=grad.mean(); if gm>1e-12:
                       v = a * np.exp(-0.5*grad/gm); s=v.sum(); if s>0: cand=v/s; update
        # If no tried or not improved, use top gradient
        if best_M_new >= M*(1-1e-12) or not tried:
            # top weights
            K=max(4,min(12,n//10?)) maybe 8
            w=_top_weights(c, K)
            # maybe combine with softmax
            T=max(M*0.05,1e-12)
            ws=_softmax_weights(c,T)
            w_all=0.5*w+0.5*ws
            for w_use in [w, w_all]:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                grad=_weighted_grad(a,w_use)
                for eta in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
                    cand=_project_simplex(a-eta*grad); update
                gm=grad.mean(); if gm>0:
                   for eta in [0.3,0.8,1.5]:
                      v=a*np.exp(-eta*grad/gm); s=v.sum(); if s>0: cand=v/s; update
            # If still no improve, try a small random direction
            if best_M_new >= M*(1-1e-12):
                for scale in [0.01,0.05,0.2]:
                    cand=_project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0,scale,n)); update

`_build_A` loops:
```
def _build_A(a):
    n=len(a)
    m=2*n-1
    A=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    if n==1: A[0,0]=a[0]; return A
    for k in range(n):
        A[k, :k+1] = a[:k+1][::-1]
    for k in range(n, m):
        L = m - k
        s = k - n + 1
        A[k, s:s+L] = a[n-1:n-L-1:-1]
    return A
```
Test n=1: m=1, first k=0 assigns A[0,:1]=a[0], second loop none. Good.
For n=2: rows k=0 row0 [a0,0]; k=1 row1 [a1,a0]; k=2 L=1,s=1 row2 [0,a1]. Conv of [a0,a1] rows: k2 a1*a1? row2 j=1 a[k-j]=a1, yes. Good.

def _random_smooth(n, rng, base=None, mix=0.5):
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    kind=int(rng.integers(6))
    if kind==0:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.4,3.0)); a=x**alpha
    elif kind==1:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.4,3.0)); a=(1.0-x)**alpha
    elif kind==2:
       c=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.9)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.4)); a=np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/(2*w*w)) + 0.05
    elif kind==3:
       a=np.ones(n); K=max(1,min(6,n//32)); for k in range(1,K+1): amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.6)/k; a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    elif kind==4:
       a=np.ones(n); for _ in range(3): c=w?; add gaussian
    else:
       a=rng.uniform(0.2,1.5,n)
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.where(finite,a,0); a=np.maximum(a,1e-4)
    if base is not None and base.size==n:
       a = max(0.2, min(0.8,mix))*base + (1-...)*a? mix parameter
    return _normalize(a)
```

def _exact_score(a):

     if a.size==0 or not np.isfinite(a).all(): return inf
     return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))

def _optimize_length(L, deadline, rng, seeds):
  if L<=0: return None, inf
  L=int(L); if L > _MAX_LEN: L=_MAX_LEN
  best_local=None; best_M=inf
  # seeds list arrays length L or produce from seed_a
  for i, seed in enumerate(seeds):
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2: break
      a = seed if len==L else _resample(seed,L)
      sub_pct = ... allocate remaining proportionally to initial rank: first seed gets more time
      sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + (fraction)*remaining)
      a_local, M_local = _local_refine(a, sub_deadline, np.random.default_rng(rng.integers(2**63)), use_lp=L<=512 and sub_deadline-now>20, lp_every=2 if L<=300 else 4)
      if M_local < best_M: best_local=a_local; best_M=M_local
      # additional short random init from best_local maybe
  return best_local,best_M
```

phases=[] # (L, init_array, time_fraction, use_lp)

phase_specs = [(L, 'best_upsample', frac, use_lp), (L,'parent_upsample',...), (L,'random',...), ...]
for spec in phase_specs:
   if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
   L=spec[0]; frac=spec[2]
   allowed=min(deadline - now - 5, max(5, frac * (deadline - t0) * scale?))
   if allowed < 10: continue
   a_init = _make_init(L, spec[1], best_a, parent, rng)
   if a_init is None: continue
   a_local, M_local = _local_refine(a_init, now+allowed, rng, use_lp=spec[3])
   # exact validate

`_make_init(L, kind, current_best, parent, rng)`:

Let's implement `_linprog_run(c, A_ub, b_ub, vars, deadline, remaining_frac)`:
```
def _linprog_time(c, A_ub, b_ub, deadline):
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem <= 1: return None
    tl = max(0.5, min(8.0, 0.2*rem))
    options={'time_limit': tl, 'presolve': True}
    try:
       return linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0,None), method='highs', options=options)
    except TypeError:
       try:
          return linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
       except Exception:
          return None
    except Exception:
       return None
```

```
u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
cssv = np.cumsum(u)
idx = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=float)
with np.errstate: cond = (cssv - target) / idx < u
rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
if len(rho)==0: return np.full(n, target/n)
theta = (cssv[rho[-1]] - target)/(rho[-1]+1)
w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
s=w.sum(); if s<=0 or not finite: return full; return w/s # ensure sum target
```

Now, `_resample` step:
```
def _resample(a, m):
   a=_normalize(a)
   n=a.size
   if m<=0: m=1
   m=int(m)
   if n==1:
       b=np.full(m, a[0]/m if m>0 else1); return _normalize(b)
   if m==n: return a.copy()
   if m > n:
       idx = (np.arange(m) * n) // m
       counts = np.bincount(idx, minlength=n)
       counts[counts==0]=1
       b = a[idx] / counts[idx]
   else:
       b = np.zeros(m)
       # map old i to new block center? idx_old_to_new = floor(i*m/n); but if multiple old map same, sum; some new zero.
       idx = (np.arange(n) * m) // m? wait to assign old mass to new: j = int(i*m/n)
       idx = (np.arange(n) * m) // n
       b[idx] += a  # np.add.at
       b = np.maximum(b,0)
   return _normalize(b)
```
For m>n, e.g., n=192,m=255: idx groups; mass split. Sum b = ∑ a_i =1? Because each group of count c: c*(a_i/c)=a_i. Yes. Good.
For m<n, sum b =1; some zero. Normalize.
If counts zero not for m>n? Some old may have zero count if m<n? But m>n all old at least one? If m>n, by pigeonhole some may zero if n>>m? But m>n, each old should at least one? For m>n, mapping floor(j*n/m) maybe not all old if n/m<1? Actually n=192,m=200, each old except maybe? idx values 0..189; some zero? 200 values, 192 bins, by pigeonhole all bins? Not necessarily, e.g. floor(j*0.96) increments by0 or1, could skip? Starting at0, max191, range192, number200, likely no skip but could skip if step >1? no step <1, max increment1, so no skip. So counts positive. But keep.

For m<n, using `np.add.at(b, idx, a)`, idx maybe all bins? m<n, class size>1, yes. Sum mass1. Good.

def _random_smooth(n, rng, base=None):

  if base is not None and len(base)==n:
      a = 0.5*_normalize(base) +0.5*a
  a=np.maximum(a,1e-3); return _normalize(a)
```

```
def _active_weights(c, n=None):
    m=len(c)
    if m==0: return np.zeros(0)
    M=float(c.max()); if M<=0: w=np.zeros(m); w[0]=1; return w
    T=max(M*0.05, 1e-12)
    p=np.exp(np.clip((c-M)/T, -50, 0))
    s=p.sum()
    if s>0: w_soft=p/s
    else: w_soft=np.zeros(m); w_soft[np.argmax(c)]=1
    if n:
       K=max(4, min(16, max(4, n//12)))
    else:
       K=max(4, min(16, m//16))
    K=min(K,m)
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    w_top=np.zeros(m); w_top[idx]=1.0/K
    # blend: if top all equal, maybe emphasize max by including w_soft
    return 0.6*w_top + 0.4*w_soft
```

Pseudo final code for `_local_refine`:
```
def _local_refine(a, deadline, rng, use_lp=True, lp_every=2):
    n=len(a)
    if n<2:
        a=np.ones(2)/2; n=2
    a=_normalize(a)
    c=_conv_full(a); M=float(c.max())
    best=a.copy(); best_M=M
    sigma=0.03; patience=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it+=1
        if M < best_M*(1-1e-12):
            best=a.copy(); best_M=M; patience=0; sigma=max(0.003, sigma*0.985)
        else:
            patience+=1
        if patience > 45:
            r = rng.random()
            if r < 0.5:
                a = _project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            elif r < 0.85:
                base_b = _random_smooth(n, rng, base=best)
                a = _project_simplex(0.5*best + 0.5*base_b)
            else:
                a = _random_smooth(n, rng)
            c=_conv_full(a); M=float(c.max())
            patience=0; sigma=min(0.35, sigma*1.3)
            continue
        best_point=None; best_cand_M=M; best_cand_c=c
        # LP part
        if use_lp and (it % lp_every == 0):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 8 and n <= 512:
                res = _solve_lp(a, M, deadline - 2.0, rng, with_dual=(it % 4 == 2) and n <= 448)
                if res is not None:
                    dirb, weights = res
                    if dirb is not None and np.linalg.norm(dirb-a) > 1e-7:
                        alphas = (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.8,1.0) if n <= 300 else (0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6,1.0)
                        for alpha in alphas:
                            if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                            cand = a + alpha*(dirb - a)
                            best_point,best_cand_M,best_cand_c = _try_point(cand,best_point,best_cand_M,best_cand_c)
                    if weights is not None and _has_pos(weights):
                        grad=_weighted_grad_fft(a,weights)
                        if np.linalg.norm(grad) > 1e-12:
                            etas=(0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5) if n<=300 else (0.3,0.8,1.5)
                            for eta in etas:
                                if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                                cand=_project_simplex(a - eta*grad)
                                best_point,...
                            gm=float(grad.mean())
                            if gm > 0:
                                for eta in (0.5,1.2):
                                    v = a * np.exp(-(eta*grad/gm))
                                    s=v.sum()
                                    if s>0: cand=v/s; ...
        # Gradient/top fallback if current not improved or LP not tried
        if best_point is None:
            w = _active_weights(c, n)
            if _has_pos(w):
                grad=_weighted_grad_fft(a,w)
                if np.linalg.norm(grad) > 1e-12:
                    etas=(0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0) if n<=350 else (0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0)
                    for eta in etas:
                       ...
                    gm=float(grad.mean())
                    if gm>0:
                       for eta in (0.35,0.8,1.5):
                         v=a*np.exp(-(eta*grad/gm)); ...
                    # if still none, small random from best
                    if best_point is None and patience > 10:
                       for sc in (0.005, 0.02, 0.08):
                           cand=_project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0,sc,n))
                           ...
        if best_point is not None:
            a=best_point.copy()
            c=best_cand_c
            M=best_cand_M
            # optional normalize very small drift
            s=a.sum(); if not finite or abs(s-1)>1e-8: a=_normalize(a); c=_conv_full(a); M=float(c.max())
        # else nothing
    return best,best_M
```

def _logit(a,L):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,L,endpoint=True)
   t=2*x-1
   D=6
   B=np.empty((D,L)); B[0]=1; if D>1 B[1]=t; for k...
   def from_theta(th):
       loga = B.T @ th
       loga -= loga.mean()? Actually normalization handles; but to avoid overflow, subtract max(loga) before exp.
       a=np.exp(np.clip(loga - loga.max(), -30,0))
       return _normalize(a)
   def obj(th):
       a=from_theta(th)
       c=_conv_full(a); M=float(c.max())
       T=max(M*0.1,1e-12)
       z=(c-M)/T
       z=np.clip(z,-50,0)
       s=z.sum(); if s<=0: return 2*L*M
       return 2*L*(M+T*math.log(s))
   best_a=None; best_obj=inf
   for r in range(3 if L<=512 else 1):
      th0 = rng.normal(0,1,D) * (0.5 if r else 0)
      # also try a shape from a power: create a=x**0.8; loga; fit theta = B @ a? least squares log(a) maybe
      if r>0 and r<=?

      # after, exact score, if better update

Power function:
```
def _power_init(L, alpha, endpoint='right'):
  x=np.linspace(0,1,L)
  if endpoint=='right': a=x**alpha
  else: a=(1-x)**alpha
  a=np.maximum(a,1e-6)
  return _normalize(a)
```

def _try_point(cand, best_point, best_M, best_c):
    if cand is None:
       return best_point,best_M,best_c
    cand=np.asarray(cand,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if cand.size ==0 or not np.isfinite(cand).all():
       return ...
    cc = _conv_full(cand)
    mm = float(cc.max())
    if mm < best_M * (1.0 - 1e-12):
        return cand, mm, cc
    return best_point,best_M,best_c
```

def _basis_search(L, deadline, rng):
    if L < 16: return None,inf
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem < 15: return None,inf
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,L,endpoint=True)
    t=2*x-1
    D=6
    B=np.empty((D,L), float)
    B[0]=1.0
    if D>1: B[1]=t
    if D>2:
       T2=2*t*t-1... use recurrence:
       Tk_2=np.ones(L); Tk_1=t
       for k in range(2,D): Tk=2*t*Tk_1 - Tk_2; B[k]=Tk; shift
```
Check Chebyshev T2=2t^2-1; recurrence gives 2*t*T1 - T0=2t^2-1. Good.
Destructor if D could be 1? D=6.
```
    def _reconstruct(th):
       loga = B.T @ th
       if not finite: loga=zeros
       loga = np.where(finite, loga, 0)
       loga -= loga.max()
       a=np.exp(np.clip(loga, -30, 0))
       a=np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
       return _normalize(a)
    def _soft_obj(th):
       a=_reconstruct(th)
       c=_conv_full(a); M=float(c.max())
       if M<=0: return 0.0
       T=max(M*0.05, 1e-12)
       z=(c-M)/T; z=np.clip(z,-50,0); s=float(z.sum())
       return 2.0*L*(M + T*math.log(s) if s>0 else 0.0)
    best_a=None; best_exact=inf; best_soft=inf
    starts=[]
    for alph in (0.5,1.0,1.5): starts.append(fit log power x**alph)
    starts.append(fit (1-x)**0.7)
    starts.append(rng.normal(0,0.5,D))
    maxiter = int(min(250, max(40, rem*2)))? If rem 60 =>120. Use `maxiter = int(min(220, max(40, rem*1.5)))`.
    for start in starts[:4]:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5: break
       maxit = max(20, maxiter//2?) to limit total.
       try:
          res=minimize(_soft_obj, start, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter': maxit, 'xatol':1e-5, 'fatol':1e-7, 'adapt': True})
          th = res.x if finite else start
       except: th=start
       a=_reconstruct(th); sc=_exact_score(a); if sc<best_exact: update
    return best_a,best_exact
```

Now, code for `_linprog_run`:
```
def _linprog_run(c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, deadline):
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem <= 0.5: return None
    tl = max(0.5, min(10.0, 0.25*rem))
    try:
        return linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0, None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': tl, 'presolve': True})
    except Exception:
        try:
            return linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0, None), method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
        except Exception:
            return None
```
If `time_limit` unexpected, second fallback maybe raises? Actually if first catches ValueError due unknown option, second no time_limit. Good. If highspy not available? method='highs' maybe unavailable; fallback? Could try default method. Add:
```

   try: return linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0,None))
   except: return None
```

`_solve_lp`:
```
def _solve_lp(a, M, deadline, rng, with_dual=False):
    n=len(a)
    if n<2 or n>1024 or M<=0 or time.monotonic() >= deadline: return (None,None)
    A = _build_A(a)
    m = 2*n-1
    dirb=None; weights=None
    A_scaled = A / M
    b_ub = np.ones(m)
    tie = rng.standard_normal(n)
    if n>1: tie = (tie - tie.mean()) / (np.std(tie)+1e-12)
    c_obj = -1.0 + 1e-5*tie
    res = _linprog_run(c_obj, A_scaled, b_ub, deadline)
    if res is not None and getattr(res,'success',False):
       x=np.asarray(res.x,float)
       if x.shape[0]==n and np.isfinite(x).all():
          x=np.maximum(x,0); s=x.sum()
          if s>1e-12: dirb=x/s
    if with_dual:
       # ensure using orig A
       tie2 = rng.standard_normal(m)
       if m>1: tie2 = (tie2-tie2.mean())/(np.std(tie2)+1e-12)
       c_dual = 1.0 + 1e-5*tie2
       A_dual = -A.T
       b_dual = -np.ones(n)
       res2 = _linprog_run(c_dual, A_dual, b_dual, deadline)
       if res2 is not None and getattr(res2,'success',False):
          lam=np.asarray(res2.x,float)
          if lam.shape[0]==m and np.isfinite(lam).all():
             lam=np.maximum(lam,0); s=lam.sum()
             if s>1e-12: weights=lam/s
    return dirb, weights
```
Memory: `A_scaled = A / M` creates copy. If M maybe scalar. Good. If M extremely small, copy okay. If M is 0, skip.

Tie normalized; if n=1 std 0, tie zero. Good.

Now, `_random_smooth`: Need not import random. Use rng.
```
def _random_smooth(n, rng, base=None):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   kind=int(rng.integers(0,6))
   if kind == 0:
      alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.4,2.5)); a=x**alpha
   elif kind ==1:
      alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.4,2.5)); a=(1.0-x)**alpha
   elif kind ==2:
      c=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.35)); a=np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/(2*w*w)) + 0.1
   elif kind ==3:
      a=np.ones(n); K=max(1, min(5, n//40));
      for k in range(1,K+1): amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.8/k)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x+ph)
   elif kind ==4:
      a=np.ones(n); for _ in range(rng.integers(2,4)): c=..., w=..., amp=...; a+=amp*np.exp(...)
   else:
      a=rng.uniform(0.2,1.5,n)
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.maximum(a,1e-4)
   if base is not None and len(base)==n:
      a = 0.5*_normalize(base) + 0.5*a
   return _normalize(a)
```
If kind3 K=0 for small n? n large. Use K=max(1,min(5,n//40+1)). For n=16, K=1.
`rng.integers(2,4)` returns 2 or3. Good.

Now, `_resample` downsample uses `np.add.at`. Need import numpy already.
```
def _resample(a,m):
 a=_normalize(a); n=a.size; m=int(m)
 if m<=0: return np.ones(1)
 if n==0: return np.ones(m)/m
 if n==m: return a.copy()
 if m > n:
    idx = (np.arange(m)*n)//m
    counts=np.bincount(idx,minlength=n).astype(float)
    counts[counts==0]=1.0
    b = a[idx] / counts[idx]
 else:
    b=np.zeros(m)
    idx=(np.arange(n)*m)//n
    np.add.at(b, idx, a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
 return _normalize(b)
```
For m>n with a size n, idx int. counts idx shape. a[idx] shape m. Good. For m<n, idx length n. `np.add.at` okay. Sum preserved.

    if h is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            p = np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                return p / s
    return np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64) / 192.0
...

`_normalize` uses input not list? For list? okay.
```
def _normalize(a, target=1.0):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
       arr=np.ones(1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
       return arr / s * target
    return np.full(arr.size, target / arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
```
If target=1 and size 0? no.

`_conv_full` returns c. If a has sum maybe not1. It just conv.
```
def _conv_full(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=arr.size
    if n==0: return np.array([0.0])
    nfft=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    f=rfft(arr,nfft)
    c=irfft(f*f,nfft)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c
```
For large arrays, f*f complex; irfft returns float. Good.

`_try_point` uses top-level `_conv_full`.

`_project_simplex` maybe if n=0. Use target.
```
def _project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
 v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
 n=v.size
 if n==0: return np.ones(1)
 v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0.0)
 u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
 css=np.cumsum(u)
 idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
 with np.errstate(divide='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
    cond = (css - target) / idx < u
 rho=np.nonzero(cond)[0]
 if rho.size == 0:
    return np.full(n, target/n)
 theta=(css[rho[-1]]-target)/(rho[-1]+1)
 w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
 s=float(w.sum())
 if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(n,target/n)
 return (w / s) * target
```
Check when v sum target: if all positive, cond? okay.

```
def _active_weights(c,n=None):
 m=len(c)
 if m==0: return np.zeros(0)
 M=float(c.max())
 if M<=0 or not finite: w=np.zeros(m); w[0]=1; return w
 T=max(M*0.05,1e-12)
 z=(c-M)/T; z=np.clip(z,-50,0); p=np.exp(z); s=p.sum()
 if s>0: ws=p/s else top1
 K=...

 wt=np.zeros(m); wt[idx]=1.0/K
 w=0.6*wt+0.4*ws

if K >= m: idx = np.arange(m)
else:
   kth = m - K

```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize


def _get_parent():
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            p = np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                return p / s
    return np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64) / 192.0


def _normalize(a, target=1.0):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        return (arr / s) * target
    return np.full(arr.size, target / arr.size, dtype=np.float64)


def _project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        cond = (css - target) / idx < u
    rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if rho.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (css[rho[-1]] - target) / (rho[-1] + 1)
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return (w / s) * target


def _conv_full(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    nfft = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    f = rfft(arr, nfft)
    c = irfft(f * f, nfft)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _try_point(cand, best_point, best_M, best_c):
    if cand is None:
        return best_point, best_M, best_c
    cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if cand.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
        return best_point, best_M, best_c
    cc = _conv_full(cand)
    mm = float(cc.max())
    if mm < best_M * (1.0 - 1e-12):
        return cand, mm, cc
    return best_point, best_M, best_c


def _has_pos(w):
    try:
        return bool(np.any(np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64) > 1e-15))
    except Exception:
        return False


def _resample(a, m):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if m > n:
        idx = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64) * n) // m
        counts = np.bincount(idx, minlength=n).astype(np.float64)
        counts[counts <= 0] = 1.0
        b = a[idx] / counts[idx]
    else:
        b = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64) * m) // n
        np.add.at(b, idx, a)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return _normalize(b)


def _build_A(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty((0, 0), dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(n):
        A[k, :k + 1] = a[:k + 1][::-1]
    for k in range(n, m):
        L = m - k
        s = k - n + 1
        A[k, s:s + L] = a[n - 1:n - L - 1:-1]
    return A


def _linprog_run(c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, deadline):
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem <= 0.5:
        return None
    tl = max(0.25, min(12.0, 0.25 * rem))
    try:
        return linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=(0.0, None),
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": tl, "presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        return linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=(0.0, None),
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        return linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0, None))
    except Exception:
        return None


def _solve_lp(a, M, deadline, rng, with_dual=False):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or n > 1024 or M <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None, None
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return None, None

    A = _build_A(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    dirb = None
    weights = None

    A_scaled = A / M
    tie = np.asarray(rng.standard_normal(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        tie = (tie - tie.mean()) / (np.std(tie) + 1e-12)
    c_obj = -1.0 + 1e-5 * tie
    b_ub = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    res = _linprog_run(c_obj, A_scaled, b_ub, deadline)
    if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False):
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
            s = float(x.sum())
            if s > 1e-12:
                dirb = x / s

    if with_dual and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        tie2 = np.asarray(rng.standard_normal(m), dtype=np.float64)
        if m > 1:
            tie2 = (tie2 - tie2.mean()) / (np.std(tie2) + 1e-12)
        c_dual = 1.0 + 1e-5 * tie2
        A_dual = -A.T
        b_dual = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        res2 = _linprog_run(c_dual, A_dual, b_dual, deadline)
        if res2 is not None and getattr(res2, "success", False):
            lam = np.asarray(res2.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if lam.size == m and np.all(np.isfinite(lam)):
                lam = np.maximum(lam, 0.0)
                s = float(lam.sum())
                if s > 1e-12:
                    weights = lam / s
    return dirb, weights


def _active_weights(c, n=None):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w

    T = max(M * 0.05, 1e-12)
    z = (c - M) / T
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s):
        ws = p / s
    else:
        ws = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        ws[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0

    if n is not None:
        K = max(4, min(16, max(4, n // 12)))
    else:
        K = max(4, min(16, max(4, m // 16)))
    K = min(K, m)
    if K >= m:
        idx = np.arange(m)
    else:
        kth = m - K
        idx = np.argpartition(c, kth)[kth:]
    wt = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    wt[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)

    w = 0.6 * wt + 0.4 * ws
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw > 0 and np.isfinite(sw):
        w /= sw
    else:
        w = wt
    return w


def _weighted_grad_fft(a, w):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size != need:
        if w.size > need:
            w = w[:need]
        else:
            w = np.pad(w, (0, need - w.size), mode="constant")
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = w / s

    nfft = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    fa = rfft(a, nfft)
    fw = rfft(w[::-1], nfft)
    cr = irfft(fa * fw, nfft)[:3 * n - 2]
    cr = np.where(np.isfinite(cr), cr, 0.0)
    idx = 2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n)
    g = 2.0 * cr[idx]
    return np.maximum(g, 0.0)


def _random_smooth(n, rng, base=None):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    kind = int(rng.integers(0, 6))

    if kind == 0:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 2.5))
        a = x ** alpha
    elif kind == 1:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 2.5))
        a = (1.0 - x) ** alpha
    elif kind == 2:
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        wdt = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        a = np.exp(-((x - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * wdt * wdt)) + 0.1
    elif kind == 3:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        K = max(1, min(5, n // 40 + 1))
        for k in range(1, K + 1):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.8 / k))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)
    elif kind == 4:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(int(rng.integers(2, 4))):
            c = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.9))
            wdt = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.35))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.9))
            a += amp * np.exp(-((x - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * wdt * wdt))
    else:
        a = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.5, n).astype(np.float64)

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-4)

    if base is not None and np.asarray(base).size == n:
        a = 0.5 * _normalize(base) + 0.5 * a
    return _normalize(a)


def _exact_score(a):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return float("inf")
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _basis_search(L, deadline, rng):
    L = int(L)
    if L < 16:
        return None, float("inf")
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem < 12.0:
        return None, float("inf")

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L, endpoint=True)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    D = 6
    B = np.empty((D, L), dtype=np.float64)
    B[0] = 1.0
    if D > 1:
        B[1] = t
    if D > 2:
        T0 = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        T1 = t.copy()
        for k in range(2, D):
            Tk = 2.0 * t * T1 - T0
            B[k] = Tk
            T0, T1 = T1, Tk

    def _reconstruct(th):
        th = np.asarray(th, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if th.size != D or not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
            th = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        loga = B.T @ th
        loga = np.where(np.isfinite(loga), loga, 0.0)
        loga -= float(np.max(loga))
        a = np.exp(np.clip(loga, -30.0, 0.0))
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
        return _normalize(a)

    def _soft_obj(th):
        a = _reconstruct(th)
        c = _conv_full(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0:
            return 1e3
        T = max(M * 0.05, 1e-12)
        z = (c - M) / T
        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
        s = float(z.sum())
        val = M + T * math.log(s) if s > 0 else M
        return float(2.0 * L * val)

    best_a = None
    best_exact = float("inf")

    start_candidates = []
    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5):
        p = (x ** alpha) + 1e-8
        p = _normalize(p)
        logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-12))
        try:
            th, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(B.T, logp, rcond=None)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
                start_candidates.append(th.astype(np.float64))
        except Exception:
            pass
    for alpha in (0.6, 1.2):
        p = ((1.0 - x) ** alpha) + 1e-8
        p = _normalize(p)
        logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-12))
        try:
            th, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(B.T, logp, rcond=None)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
                start_candidates.append(th.astype(np.float64))
        except Exception:
            pass
    start_candidates.append(rng.standard_normal(D).astype(np.float64))
    start_candidates.append((np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64) + rng.standard_normal(D) * 0.25))

    maxit_total = int(min(240, max(50, rem * 1.2)))
    maxit_each = max(20, maxit_total // max(1, len(start_candidates)))

    for st in start_candidates[:4]:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 4.0:
            break
        st = np.asarray(st, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if st.size != D or not np.all(np.isfinite(st)):
            continue
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _soft_obj,
                st,
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={"maxiter": maxit_each, "xatol": 1e-5, "fatol": 1e-7},
            )
            th = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64) if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None else st
        except Exception:
            th = st
        a = _reconstruct(th)
        sc = _exact_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_exact


def _local_refine(a, deadline, rng, use_lp=True, lp_every=2):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        a = np.ones(2, dtype=np.float64) / 2.0
        n = 2

    c = _conv_full(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    sigma = 0.03
    patience = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        if M < best_M * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best = a.copy()
            best_M = M
            patience = 0
            sigma = max(0.003, sigma * 0.985)
        else:
            patience += 1

        if patience > 45:
            r = float(rng.random())
            if r < 0.5:
                a = _project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            elif r < 0.85:
                base_b = _random_smooth(n, rng, base=best)
                a = _project_simplex(0.5 * best + 0.5 * base_b)
            else:
                a = _random_smooth(n, rng)
            c = _conv_full(a)
            M = float(c.max())
            patience = 0
            sigma = min(0.35, sigma * 1.3)
            continue

        best_point = None
        best_cand_M = M
        best_cand_c = c

        if use_lp and (it % lp_every == 0):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 8.0 and n <= 512:
                try:
                    dirb, weights = _solve_lp(
                        a,
                        M,
                        deadline - 2.0,
                        rng,
                        with_dual=(it % 4 == 2) and n <= 448,
                    )
                except Exception:
                    dirb, weights = None, None

                if dirb is not None:
                    if np.any(np.abs(dirb - a) > 1e-7):
                        if n <= 300:
                            alphas = (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.55, 0.8, 1.0)
                        else:
                            alphas = (0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.6, 1.0)
                        for alpha in alphas:
                            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                                break
                            cand = a + alpha * (dirb - a)
                            best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c = _try_point(
                                cand, best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c
                            )

                if weights is not None and _has_pos(weights):
                    grad = _weighted_grad_fft(a, weights)
                    if float(np.linalg.norm(grad)) > 1e-12:
                        etas = (0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1.5) if n <= 300 else (0.3, 0.8, 1.5)
                        for eta in etas:
                            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                                break
                            cand = _project_simplex(a - eta * grad)
                            best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c = _try_point(
                                cand, best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c
                            )
                        gm = float(grad.mean())
                        if gm > 1e-18:
                            for eta in (0.5, 1.2):
                                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                                    break
                                v = a * np.exp(-(eta * grad) / gm)
                                s = float(v.sum())
                                if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s):
                                    cand = v / s
                                    best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c = _try_point(
                                        cand, best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c
                                    )

        if best_point is None:
            w = _active_weights(c, n)
            if _has_pos(w):
                grad = _weighted_grad_fft(a, w)
                if float(np.linalg.norm(grad)) > 1e-12:
                    if n <= 350:
                        etas = (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)
                    else:
                        etas = (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)
                    for eta in etas:
                        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                            break
                        cand = _project_simplex(a - eta * grad)
                        best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c = _try_point(
                            cand, best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c
                        )

                    gm = float(grad.mean())
                    if gm > 1e-18:
                        for eta in (0.35, 0.8, 1.5):
                            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                                break
                            v = a * np.exp(-(eta * grad) / gm)
                            s = float(v.sum())
                            if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s):
                                cand = v / s
                                best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c = _try_point(
                                    cand, best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c
                                )

                    if best_point is None and patience > 10:
                        for sc in (0.005, 0.02, 0.08):
                            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                                break
                            cand = _project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, sc, n))
                            best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c = _try_point(
                                cand, best_point, best_cand_M, best_cand_c
                            )

        if best_point is not None:
            a = np.asarray(best_point, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            c = np.asarray(best_cand_c, dtype=np.float64)
            M = float(best_cand_M)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-8:
                a = _normalize(a)
                c = _conv_full(a)
                M = float(c.max())

    return best, best_M


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0

    if budget <= 1.0:
        try:
            p = _get_parent()
            sc = _exact_score(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                return [float(x) for x in p]
        except Exception:
            pass
        return [1.0]

    head = max(5.0, min(20.0, 0.02 * budget))
    deadline = t0 + budget - head
    if deadline <= t0 + 2.0:
        deadline = t0 + max(2.0, budget * 0.9)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if np.isfinite(float(seed)) else 42)

    parent = _get_parent()
    best_a = _normalize(parent)
    best_score = _exact_score(best_a)

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
        best_score = _exact_score(best_a)
        if not np.isfinite(best_score):
            return [1.0]

    if budget > 220.0 and deadline - time.monotonic() > 130.0:
        basis_time = min(50.0, max(15.0, 0.04 * budget))
        basis_deadline = time.monotonic() + basis_time
        if basis_deadline < deadline - 30.0:
            try:
                ba, bsc = _basis_search(256, basis_deadline, rng)
                if ba is not None and np.isfinite(bsc) and bsc < best_score:
                    best_a = _normalize(ba)
                    best_score = float(bsc)
            except Exception:
                pass

    pL = int(best_a.size) if best_a.size > 0 else 192
    if pL < 1:
        pL = 192

    order = []
    for L in (256, 384, 512):
        if 32 <= L <= 1024:
            order.append(L)
    if pL not in order and 8 <= pL <= 1024:
        order.append(pL)
    if not order:
        order = [max(16, min(1024, pL))]

    weights = {}
    for L in order:
        if L == 256:
            w = 1.0
        elif L == 384:
            w = 1.2
        elif L == 512:
            w = 0.8
        elif L == pL:
            w = 0.55
        else:
            w = 0.5
        if L > 768:
            w *= 0.5
        if L < 64:
            w *= 0.3
        weights[L] = weights.get(L, 0.0) + w

    total_w = sum(weights.values())
    if total_w <= 0:
        total_w = 1.0
    phase_budget = max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.95)

    for L in order:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
            break
        timeL = (weights.get(L, 0.5) / total_w) * phase_budget
        timeL = min(timeL, deadline - time.monotonic() - 18.0)
        if timeL < 12.0:
            continue

        Lint = int(L)
        if Lint < 2:
            continue

        use_lp = (Lint <= 512 and timeL > 55.0)
        if Lint <= 300:
            lp_every = 2
        elif Lint <= 420:
            lp_every = 3
        else:
            lp_every = 4

        # Main run seeded from current best shape.
        subA = timeL * (0.75 if timeL > 70.0 else 1.0)
        subA = min(subA, deadline - time.monotonic() - 18.0)
        if subA > 10.0:
            try:
                initA = _resample(best_a, Lint)
                locA, ML = _local_refine(
                    initA,
                    time.monotonic() + subA,
                    rng,
                    use_lp=use_lp,
                    lp_every=lp_every,
                )
                score_fft = 2.0 * int(locA.size) * float(ML)
                if np.isfinite(score_fft) and score_fft < best_score + 1e-6:
                    sc = _exact_score(locA)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_a = _normalize(locA)
                        best_score = float(sc)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Secondary exploratory run.
        rem_after = deadline - time.monotonic() - 18.0
        if (timeL - subA) > 14.0 and rem_after > 18.0:
            subB = min(timeL * 0.25, rem_after)
            if subB > 10.0:
                try:
                    baseSeed = _resample(best_a, Lint)
                    initB = _random_smooth(Lint, rng, base=baseSeed)
                    use_lp2 = use_lp and subB > 45.0
                    locB, MLB = _local_refine(
                        initB,
                        time.monotonic() + subB,
                        rng,
                        use_lp=use_lp2,
                        lp_every=lp_every,
                    )
                    score_fft = 2.0 * int(locB.size) * float(MLB)
                    if np.isfinite(score_fft) and score_fft < best_score + 1e-6:
                        sc = _exact_score(locB)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                            best_a = _normalize(locB)
                            best_score = float(sc)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Final polish on the best exact-scoring candidate.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        pol_time = min(220.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 12.0)
        if pol_time > 25.0:
            try:
                nbest = int(best_a.size)
                use_lp_final = nbest <= 512 and pol_time > 70.0
                lp_every_final = 3
                locF, MLF = _local_refine(
                    best_a,
                    time.monotonic() + pol_time,
                    rng,
                    use_lp=use_lp_final,
                    lp_every=lp_every_final,
                )
                score_fft = 2.0 * int(locF.size) * float(MLF)
                if np.isfinite(score_fft) and score_fft < best_score + 1e-6:
                    sc = _exact_score(locF)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_a = _normalize(locF)
                        best_score = float(sc)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final validation.
    try:
        best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if best_a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)):
            raise ValueError
        best_a = np.maximum(best_a, 0.0)
        best_a = _normalize(best_a)
        final_score = _exact_score(best_a)
        if np.isfinite(final_score) and final_score < best_score:
            best_score = final_score
    except Exception:
        final_score = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        try:
            fb = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
            fs = _exact_score(fb)
            if np.isfinite(fs):
                return [float(x) for x in fb]
        except Exception:
            pass
        return [1.0]

    out = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = _normalize(out)
    if out.size == 0:
        return [1.0]
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
